# Translation suspects (read-only scan)

Generated by `scripts/scan_translation_suspects.py` from `data/external/indic/translated/translated_final_2026-10-07.csv` (the kept translations, each next to its English parent). Nothing in `data/`, `split_map.csv`, `models/` or the test set was read for labels or changed; this report only lists rows for you to review. **Heuristic**: a hit means "look at it", not "wrong".

Reason codes: **a** number / amount / % / URL missing or different · **b** unit or time word has no counterpart (per month, daily, only, free, within N hours, N hours/days) · **c** capitalised word or ALL-CAPS phrase not kept as written (high = non-dictionary / CamelCase word, low = ordinary English word in capitals; some are legitimate transliterations such as Netflix → नेटफ्लिक्स; capitalised dictionary words like Bank or Amazon are not checked) · **d** length under 40% or over 250% of the source · **e** identical or empty.

A row can carry several codes, so the code counts add up to more than the row count. Median length ratio (translation / source, characters): hindi 1.05, kannada 1.15.

Code c rows by confidence (high = a non-dictionary or CamelCase word such as Licious, StanChart; low = an ordinary English word in capitals such as URGENT, NEVER): hindi high: 281; hindi low: 19; kannada high: 271; kannada low: 16.
